First let's take the twelve types of mental movement had with the four instants known as \"the mastery of phenomena,\" as well as eight instants of the uninterrupted path [part of the path of seeing, the direct perception of selflessness] which is free of desire. These are the deeds that act to bring black deeds to an end, for they function to eliminate all the mental afflictions that relate to the desire realm."

An instance where this stage comes from dislike would be where you kill your enemy. And a case where the undertaking stage comes from being ignorant of things would be where you kill someone like your father or mother out of some meritorious intent."

===== english/322/report.md =====
# C05:322 — Codex independent English-first proposal
Challenge the discontinuous second ending causative first: zad/end and byed/act retain only separable atoms; bring is unwrapped. ZHI/four is omitted with a PROBABLE spelling question.
All complete source rows and neighboring316–330 context were read and checked against the original read-only spine; source origin is af139c573f85aaf33278cd079719947ee9af8cdd through315, not a future acceptance baseline.
Counts: 27 spans; 19 non-null d5 pairs; 19 total d5; 6 d7; 0 nulls; 1 proposed errata.
decisions.json records every source/English range, selected occurrence, rationale and material omission. Unresolved/distributed expressions are omitted with reasons, never converted into false nulls.
Complete HGM objects are in ../evidence/lexical-evidence.json and supplemental-lexical-evidence.json; complete corpus objects and counted query parameters are indexed in ../evidence/query-index.json.
Physical full bytes and complete reversible field proofs are preserved once under ../evidence/. All five refutation grounds for all five categories are in five-ground-errata-review.json; original194-entry register remains unchanged.
Actual final generator: EXIT=0; stdout/body=4308 bytes; stderr=0 bytes; SHA256=efbd6580eecfd72657bc81f0781e0bccf4bf0e27cbdce635d0c15f60b578f13b.
Canonical resolver: EXIT=0, empty stderr, all intended ranges exact. Native input/output/error/exit and execution records: ../native/322-generator-v1 and ../native/322-resolver-v1.
One canonical generation and one canonical resolver run for this unchanged final version; both passed. No semantic acceptance is implied, and no hgm_gloss or production file was changed.

===== tibetan/323/report.md =====
# C05:323 — Codex independent Tibetan-first proposal
Original source origin: af139c573f85aaf33278cd079719947ee9af8cdd through315; no future acceptance baseline was consumed.
Challenge bral ba → eliminating first: it is the separate means predicate before main spong; both finite eliminates occurrences are source-specific.
Counts: 27 spans; 20 non-null d5 / 20 total d5; 4 members; 0 nulls.
Errata: 0 PROBABLE candidates; five-category/five-ground screen in errata-audit.json; following-heading class not re-filed.
Every proposed span has exact measured source/English ranges and a local rationale in decisions.json; unresolved material is explicitly omitted, not nulled.
Complete master objects, original corpus query rows, physical source and pinned governance are indexed once in ../evidence/.
Canonical generator: EXIT=0; stderr bytes=0; body byte count=4188; SHA256=94b270b97b073e4c4451cad9020accfffaaaf22e04e19e20f9dfd335406e38df.
Canonical resolver: EXIT=0; stderr bytes=0; every resolved range equals independent author intent. Native input/output/error/exit remain in generator-v1/ and resolver-v1/.
All three final segments generated exactly once; no passing generation rerun for packaging. Mechanical success is not semantic acceptance.
Read-only setup encountered two schema/physical-layout assumption failures before proposal generation; original methods and truthful incident records are preserved in ../helpers/ and ../evidence/.
Source/master/repository remain untouched; all work is provisional and never promoted into hgm_gloss.

===== english/323/report.md =====
# C05:323 — Codex independent English-first proposal
Challenge bral ba/eliminating first: the local freedom-from-desire frame is an active elimination recast, distinct from both spong/eliminates occurrences. thob pa owns hold once, not the defining relative too.
All complete source rows and neighboring316–330 context were read and checked against the original read-only spine; source origin is af139c573f85aaf33278cd079719947ee9af8cdd through315, not a future acceptance baseline.
Counts: 26 spans; 22 non-null d5 pairs; 22 total d5; 2 d7; 0 nulls; 0 proposed errata.
decisions.json records every source/English range, selected occurrence, rationale and material omission. Unresolved/distributed expressions are omitted with reasons, never converted into false nulls.
Complete HGM objects are in ../evidence/lexical-evidence.json and supplemental-lexical-evidence.json; complete corpus objects and counted query parameters are indexed in ../evidence/query-index.json.
Physical full bytes and complete reversible field proofs are preserved once under ../evidence/. All five refutation grounds for all five categories are in five-ground-errata-review.json; original194-entry register remains unchanged.
Actual final generator: EXIT=0; stdout/body=4189 bytes; stderr=0 bytes; SHA256=520d6f028ad18c04b4ba8e5ec84d486956fec6523a322a2e5707c41c2144647e.
Canonical resolver: EXIT=0, empty stderr, all intended ranges exact. Native input/output/error/exit and execution records: ../native/323-generator-v1 and ../native/323-resolver-v1.
One canonical generation and one canonical resolver run for this unchanged final version; both passed. No semantic acceptance is implied, and no hgm_gloss or production file was changed.

===== reconciled/323/report.md =====
# C05:323 — independent Codex reconciliation
PROVISIONAL; produced by Codex. This role does not accept or land pages.
Source/governance origin: af139c573f85aaf33278cd079719947ee9af8cdd, through 315. The later through-321 acceptance baseline was not consumed.
Both frozen originals are complete; 53 original span dispositions and all original omissions are recorded without overwriting their evidence.
Recast three isolated aspiration/desire/bral atoms into the complete d3 release relation; retain la/for, dkar gnag with members, gang yin pa at d3, and established sgo nas at d5.
Counts: 26 spans; 18 non-null d5; 4 d7; 2 d3; 2 d6; 0 nulls.
Full ordered seven-key tuples, canonical occurrences, depth checks and exact unwrapped extents are separate structured records.
Each med stays inside its uninterrupted-path modifier; no negative affix or false null is introduced.
Gated d5/d7 heads: 0. Exact proposed allowance: {}. No allowance file or gate was changed.
Errata: 0 LOW/PROBABLE editorial question(s); ten original five-ground screens rechecked for this segment. Source and GMR wording remain verbatim.
Co-occurrence evidence includes ILL:3838 supporting active release wording only in its different complete relation; 15 substring hits are not 15 attestations of the isolated pairing.
All six original successful generator proofs were reused only after exact frozen spec/stdin, body/stdout, stderr, exit, pinned-code and source identity checks.
Two fresh original resolvers and one final resolver agree with the complete declared ranges. Each final generator ran exactly once.
FINAL GENERATOR: EXIT=0; stderr bytes=0; body byte count=4381.

===== english/324/report.md =====
# C05:324 — Codex independent English-first proposal
Challenge the realm discrepancy first: gzugs/formless realm is unbanked despite that exact HGM glossary entry. LOW/PROBABLE editorial review preserves counterevidence and source bytes.
All complete source rows and neighboring316–330 context were read and checked against the original read-only spine; source origin is af139c573f85aaf33278cd079719947ee9af8cdd through315, not a future acceptance baseline.
Counts: 20 spans; 13 non-null d5 pairs; 13 total d5; 4 d7; 0 nulls; 1 proposed errata.
decisions.json records every source/English range, selected occurrence, rationale and material omission. Unresolved/distributed expressions are omitted with reasons, never converted into false nulls.
Complete HGM objects are in ../evidence/lexical-evidence.json and supplemental-lexical-evidence.json; complete corpus objects and counted query parameters are indexed in ../evidence/query-index.json.
Physical full bytes and complete reversible field proofs are preserved once under ../evidence/. All five refutation grounds for all five categories are in five-ground-errata-review.json; original194-entry register remains unchanged.
Actual final generator: EXIT=0; stdout/body=3505 bytes; stderr=0 bytes; SHA256=d7a693c94fa02e8cb67b89772f6cc45b9aaab185af43d67e9d0b4ada32044077.
Canonical resolver: EXIT=0, empty stderr, all intended ranges exact. Native input/output/error/exit and execution records: ../native/324-generator-v1 and ../native/324-resolver-v1.
One canonical generation and one canonical resolver run for this unchanged final version; both passed. No semantic acceptance is implied, and no hgm_gloss or production file was changed.
